#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

+bool parseMatrixSize(const char* text, size_t* const value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '\0' || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;

+void checkMpi(const int status, const char* where) {

+void checkMpi(const int status, const char* where) {

codex
The first build exposed a CMake linker detail: CMake selects the C++ driver for the final link, so a CUDA-driver-only link flag is invalid there. I’m removing that flag; OpenMP’s imported target already supplies the correct link option.
apply patch

+    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t extraRows = N % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t localRows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(worldRank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(worldRank) * baseRows +
+                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(worldRank), extraRows);
+    const size_t matrixElements = N * N;
+    const size_t localElements = localRows * N;
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
+        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", worldSize);
+        std::printf("OpenMP threads per rank: %d\n", omp_get_max_threads());
+        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
+    }
+
+    // A is distributed by contiguous rows. B is replicated, which avoids a
+    // communication dependency in the DGEMM critical path and lets every GPU
+    // execute an optimized square/rectangular DGEMM independently.
+    PinnedBuffer hostA(localElements);
+    PinnedBuffer hostB(matrixElements);
+    initMatrixRows(hostA.data(), N, firstRow, localRows);
+    initMatrixRows(hostB.data(), N, 0, N);
+
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    double* deviceB = nullptr;
+    double* deviceC = nullptr;
+    if (localElements != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceA), localElements * sizeof(double)),
+                  "cudaMalloc(A)");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceC), localElements * sizeof(double)),
+                  "cudaMalloc(C)");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceB), matrixElements * sizeof(double)),
+              "cudaMalloc(B)");
+
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+    cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+    checkCublas(cublasCreate(&handle), "cublasCreate");
+    checkCublas(cublasSetStream(handle, stream), "cublasSetStream");
+    checkCublas(cublasSetMathMode(handle, CUBLAS_DEFAULT_MATH), "cublasSetMathMode");
+
+    // Matrix construction is outside the serial benchmark's timed region, so
+    // transfer the immutable inputs before timing the distributed DGEMMs too.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceB, hostB.data(), matrixElements * sizeof(double),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+              "cudaMemcpyAsync(B)");
+    if (localElements != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceA, hostA.data(), localElements * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+                  "cudaMemcpyAsync(A)");
     }
-    
-    // Validation
-    if (validate) {
-        printf("Validating result...\n");
-        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
-        
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize(inputs)");
+
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent), "cudaEventCreate(start)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent), "cudaEventCreate(stop)");
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
+    }
+    checkMpi(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Barrier");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(startEvent, stream), "cudaEventRecord(start)");
+
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        // The row-major product C=A*B is the column-major product
+        // C^T=B^T*A^T.  This mapping avoids expensive explicit transposes and
+        // dispatches directly to the vendor-tuned CUDA DGEMM implementation.
+        const double alpha = 1.0;
+        const double beta = 0.0;
+        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                 static_cast<int>(N), static_cast<int>(localRows),
+                                 static_cast<int>(N), &alpha, deviceB, static_cast<int>(N),
+                                 deviceA, static_cast<int>(N), &beta, deviceC,
+                                 static_cast<int>(N)),
+                    "cublasDgemm");
+    }
+
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent, stream), "cudaEventRecord(stop)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent), "cudaEventSynchronize(stop)");
+    float localMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&localMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent),
+              "cudaEventElapsedTime");
+
+    double maximumMilliseconds = 0.0;
+    const double localMillisecondsDouble = static_cast<double>(localMilliseconds);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Reduce(&localMillisecondsDouble, &maximumMilliseconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX,
+                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+             "MPI_Reduce(timing)");
+
+    const bool needFullResult = printResults || validate;
+    PinnedBuffer localResult(needFullResult ? localElements : 0);
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> receiveDisplacements;
+    if (needFullResult) {
+        if (localElements != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(localResult.data(), deviceC, localElements * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C)");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize(result)");
+        }
+
+        if (worldRank == 0) {
+            result.resize(matrixElements);
+            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            receiveDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            size_t displacement = 0;
+            for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+                const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+                const size_t count = rows * N;
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+                receiveDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+                displacement += count;
+            }
+        }
+
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(localResult.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? result.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveDisplacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                             MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv(C)");
+    }
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", maximumMilliseconds);
+        const double gflops = maximumMilliseconds > 0.0
+                                  ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                                     static_cast<double>(N)) /
+                                        (maximumMilliseconds * 1.0e6)
+                                  : 0.0;
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+        }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (!validateResult(result, N)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                checkMpi(MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE), "MPI_Abort(validation)");
+                return EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(start)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(stop)");
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy");
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+    if (deviceA != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)");
+    if (deviceC != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)");
+    }
+
+    checkMpi(MPI_Finalize(), "MPI_Finalize");
+    return EXIT_SUCCESS;
 }

+bool parseMatrixSize(const char* text, size_t* const value) {

-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;

+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent), "cudaEventCreate(start)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent), "cudaEventCreate(stop)");
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
+    }
+    checkMpi(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Barrier");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(startEvent, stream), "cudaEventRecord(start)");
+
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        // The row-major product C=A*B is the column-major product
+        // C^T=B^T*A^T.  This mapping avoids expensive explicit transposes and
+        // dispatches directly to the vendor-tuned CUDA DGEMM implementation.
+        const double alpha = 1.0;
+        const double beta = 0.0;

+                                 static_cast<int>(N), static_cast<int>(localRows),
+                                 static_cast<int>(N), &alpha, deviceB, static_cast<int>(N),
+                                 deviceA, static_cast<int>(N), &beta, deviceC,
+                                 static_cast<int>(N)),
+                    "cublasDgemm");
+    }
+

+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent), "cudaEventSynchronize(stop)");

+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&localMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent),
+              "cudaEventElapsedTime");

+    double maximumMilliseconds = 0.0;
+    const double localMillisecondsDouble = static_cast<double>(localMilliseconds);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Reduce(&localMillisecondsDouble, &maximumMilliseconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX,
+                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+             "MPI_Reduce(timing)");

+    const bool needFullResult = printResults || validate;
+    PinnedBuffer localResult(needFullResult ? localElements : 0);
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> receiveDisplacements;
+    if (needFullResult) {
+        if (localElements != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(localResult.data(), deviceC, localElements * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C)");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize(result)");
+        }
+
+        if (worldRank == 0) {
+            result.resize(matrixElements);
+            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            receiveDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            size_t displacement = 0;
+            for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+                const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+                const size_t count = rows * N;
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+                receiveDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+                displacement += count;
+            }
+        }
+
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(localResult.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? result.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveDisplacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                             MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv(C)");
+    }
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", maximumMilliseconds);
+        const double gflops = maximumMilliseconds > 0.0
+                                  ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                                     static_cast<double>(N)) /
+                                        (maximumMilliseconds * 1.0e6)
+                                  : 0.0;
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+        }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (!validateResult(result, N)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                checkMpi(MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE), "MPI_Abort(validation)");
+                return EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(start)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(stop)");
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy");
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+    if (deviceA != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)");
+    if (deviceC != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)");
+    }
+
+    checkMpi(MPI_Finalize(), "MPI_Finalize");
+    return EXIT_SUCCESS;

-            return 0;
-        } else {

-            return 1;
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize(inputs)");

+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent), "cudaEventCreate(start)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent), "cudaEventCreate(stop)");
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
+    }
+    checkMpi(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Barrier");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(startEvent, stream), "cudaEventRecord(start)");
+
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        // The row-major product C=A*B is the column-major product
+        // C^T=B^T*A^T.  This mapping avoids expensive explicit transposes and
+        // dispatches directly to the vendor-tuned CUDA DGEMM implementation.
+        const double alpha = 1.0;
+        const double beta = 0.0;
+        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                 static_cast<int>(N), static_cast<int>(localRows),
+                                 static_cast<int>(N), &alpha, deviceB, static_cast<int>(N),
+                                 deviceA, static_cast<int>(N), &beta, deviceC,
+                                 static_cast<int>(N)),
+                    "cublasDgemm");
+    }

+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent), "cudaEventSynchronize(stop)");

+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&localMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent),
+              "cudaEventElapsedTime");
+
+    double maximumMilliseconds = 0.0;
+    const double localMillisecondsDouble = static_cast<double>(localMilliseconds);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Reduce(&localMillisecondsDouble, &maximumMilliseconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX,
+                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+             "MPI_Reduce(timing)");
+
+    const bool needFullResult = printResults || validate;
+    PinnedBuffer localResult(needFullResult ? localElements : 0);
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> receiveDisplacements;
+    if (needFullResult) {
+        if (localElements != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(localResult.data(), deviceC, localElements * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C)");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize(result)");
+        }
+
+        if (worldRank == 0) {
+            result.resize(matrixElements);
+            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            receiveDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            size_t displacement = 0;
+            for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+                const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+                const size_t count = rows * N;
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+                receiveDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+                displacement += count;
+            }
+        }
+
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(localResult.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? result.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveDisplacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                             MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv(C)");
+    }
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", maximumMilliseconds);
+        const double gflops = maximumMilliseconds > 0.0
+                                  ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                                     static_cast<double>(N)) /
+                                        (maximumMilliseconds * 1.0e6)
+                                  : 0.0;
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+        }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (!validateResult(result, N)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                checkMpi(MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE), "MPI_Abort(validation)");
+                return EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(start)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(stop)");
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy");
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+    if (deviceA != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)");
+    if (deviceC != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)");
+    }
+
+    checkMpi(MPI_Finalize(), "MPI_Finalize");
+    return EXIT_SUCCESS;
 }

+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent), "cudaEventCreate(stop)");

+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(startEvent, stream), "cudaEventRecord(start)");

+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        // The row-major product C=A*B is the column-major product
+        // C^T=B^T*A^T.  This mapping avoids expensive explicit transposes and
+        // dispatches directly to the vendor-tuned CUDA DGEMM implementation.

+        const double beta = 0.0;

+    }

+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent), "cudaEventSynchronize(stop)");

+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&localMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent),
+              "cudaEventElapsedTime");
+
+    double maximumMilliseconds = 0.0;
+    const double localMillisecondsDouble = static_cast<double>(localMilliseconds);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Reduce(&localMillisecondsDouble, &maximumMilliseconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX,
+                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+             "MPI_Reduce(timing)");
+
+    const bool needFullResult = printResults || validate;
+    PinnedBuffer localResult(needFullResult ? localElements : 0);
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> receiveDisplacements;
+    if (needFullResult) {
+        if (localElements != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(localResult.data(), deviceC, localElements * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C)");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize(result)");
+        }
+
+        if (worldRank == 0) {
+            result.resize(matrixElements);
+            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            receiveDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            size_t displacement = 0;
+            for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+                const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+                const size_t count = rows * N;
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+                receiveDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+                displacement += count;
+            }
+        }
+
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(localResult.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? result.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveDisplacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                             MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv(C)");
+    }
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", maximumMilliseconds);
+        const double gflops = maximumMilliseconds > 0.0
+                                  ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                                     static_cast<double>(N)) /
+                                        (maximumMilliseconds * 1.0e6)
+                                  : 0.0;
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+        }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (!validateResult(result, N)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                checkMpi(MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE), "MPI_Abort(validation)");
+                return EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
         }
     }

-    return 0;

+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(start)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(stop)");
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy");
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+    if (deviceA != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)");
+    if (deviceC != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)");
+    }
+
+    checkMpi(MPI_Finalize(), "MPI_Finalize");
+    return EXIT_SUCCESS;
 }

+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
+    }
+    checkMpi(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Barrier");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(startEvent, stream), "cudaEventRecord(start)");
+
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        // The row-major product C=A*B is the column-major product
+        // C^T=B^T*A^T.  This mapping avoids expensive explicit transposes and
+        // dispatches directly to the vendor-tuned CUDA DGEMM implementation.
+        const double alpha = 1.0;
+        const double beta = 0.0;
+        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                 static_cast<int>(N), static_cast<int>(localRows),
+                                 static_cast<int>(N), &alpha, deviceB, static_cast<int>(N),
+                                 deviceA, static_cast<int>(N), &beta, deviceC,
+                                 static_cast<int>(N)),
+                    "cublasDgemm");
+    }
+
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent, stream), "cudaEventRecord(stop)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent), "cudaEventSynchronize(stop)");
+    float localMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&localMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent),
+              "cudaEventElapsedTime");
+
+    double maximumMilliseconds = 0.0;
+    const double localMillisecondsDouble = static_cast<double>(localMilliseconds);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Reduce(&localMillisecondsDouble, &maximumMilliseconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX,
+                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+             "MPI_Reduce(timing)");
+
+    const bool needFullResult = printResults || validate;
+    PinnedBuffer localResult(needFullResult ? localElements : 0);
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> receiveDisplacements;
+    if (needFullResult) {
+        if (localElements != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(localResult.data(), deviceC, localElements * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C)");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize(result)");
+        }
+
+        if (worldRank == 0) {
+            result.resize(matrixElements);
+            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            receiveDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            size_t displacement = 0;
+            for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+                const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+                const size_t count = rows * N;
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+                receiveDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+                displacement += count;
+            }
+        }
+
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(localResult.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? result.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveDisplacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                             MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv(C)");
+    }
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", maximumMilliseconds);
+        const double gflops = maximumMilliseconds > 0.0
+                                  ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                                     static_cast<double>(N)) /
+                                        (maximumMilliseconds * 1.0e6)
+                                  : 0.0;
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+        }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (!validateResult(result, N)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                checkMpi(MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE), "MPI_Abort(validation)");
+                return EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(start)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(stop)");
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy");
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+    if (deviceA != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)");
+    if (deviceC != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)");
+    }
+
+    checkMpi(MPI_Finalize(), "MPI_Finalize");
+    return EXIT_SUCCESS;
 }

+    const size_t localRows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(worldRank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(worldRank) * baseRows +
+                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(worldRank), extraRows);
+    const size_t matrixElements = N * N;
+    const size_t localElements = localRows * N;
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
+        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", worldSize);
+        std::printf("OpenMP threads per rank: %d\n", omp_get_max_threads());
+        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
+    }
+
+    // A is distributed by contiguous rows. B is replicated, which avoids a
+    // communication dependency in the DGEMM critical path and lets every GPU
+    // execute an optimized square/rectangular DGEMM independently.
+    PinnedBuffer hostA(localElements);
+    PinnedBuffer hostB(matrixElements);
+    initMatrixRows(hostA.data(), N, firstRow, localRows);
+    initMatrixRows(hostB.data(), N, 0, N);
+
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    double* deviceB = nullptr;
+    double* deviceC = nullptr;
+    if (localElements != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceA), localElements * sizeof(double)),
+                  "cudaMalloc(A)");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceC), localElements * sizeof(double)),
+                  "cudaMalloc(C)");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceB), matrixElements * sizeof(double)),
+              "cudaMalloc(B)");
+
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+    cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+    checkCublas(cublasCreate(&handle), "cublasCreate");
+    checkCublas(cublasSetStream(handle, stream), "cublasSetStream");
+    checkCublas(cublasSetMathMode(handle, CUBLAS_DEFAULT_MATH), "cublasSetMathMode");
+
+    // Matrix construction is outside the serial benchmark's timed region, so
+    // transfer the immutable inputs before timing the distributed DGEMMs too.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceB, hostB.data(), matrixElements * sizeof(double),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+              "cudaMemcpyAsync(B)");
+    if (localElements != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceA, hostA.data(), localElements * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+                  "cudaMemcpyAsync(A)");
     }
-    
-    // Validation
-    if (validate) {
-        printf("Validating result...\n");
-        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
-        
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize(inputs)");
+
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent), "cudaEventCreate(start)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent), "cudaEventCreate(stop)");
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
+    }
+    checkMpi(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD), "MPI_Barrier");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(startEvent, stream), "cudaEventRecord(start)");
+
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        // The row-major product C=A*B is the column-major product
+        // C^T=B^T*A^T.  This mapping avoids expensive explicit transposes and
+        // dispatches directly to the vendor-tuned CUDA DGEMM implementation.
+        const double alpha = 1.0;
+        const double beta = 0.0;
+        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                 static_cast<int>(N), static_cast<int>(localRows),
+                                 static_cast<int>(N), &alpha, deviceB, static_cast<int>(N),
+                                 deviceA, static_cast<int>(N), &beta, deviceC,
+                                 static_cast<int>(N)),
+                    "cublasDgemm");
+    }
+
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent, stream), "cudaEventRecord(stop)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent), "cudaEventSynchronize(stop)");
+    float localMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&localMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent),
+              "cudaEventElapsedTime");
+
+    double maximumMilliseconds = 0.0;
+    const double localMillisecondsDouble = static_cast<double>(localMilliseconds);
+    checkMpi(MPI_Reduce(&localMillisecondsDouble, &maximumMilliseconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX,
+                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+             "MPI_Reduce(timing)");
+
+    const bool needFullResult = printResults || validate;
+    PinnedBuffer localResult(needFullResult ? localElements : 0);
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> receiveDisplacements;
+    if (needFullResult) {
+        if (localElements != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(localResult.data(), deviceC, localElements * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C)");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize(result)");
+        }
+
+        if (worldRank == 0) {
+            result.resize(matrixElements);
+            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            receiveDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            size_t displacement = 0;
+            for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+                const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+                const size_t count = rows * N;
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+                receiveDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+                displacement += count;
+            }
+        }
+
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(localResult.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? result.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveDisplacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                             MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv(C)");
+    }
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", maximumMilliseconds);
+        const double gflops = maximumMilliseconds > 0.0
+                                  ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                                     static_cast<double>(N)) /
+                                        (maximumMilliseconds * 1.0e6)
+                                  : 0.0;
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+        }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (!validateResult(result, N)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                checkMpi(MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE), "MPI_Abort(validation)");
+                return EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(start)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(stop)");
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy");
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+    if (deviceA != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)");
+    if (deviceC != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)");
+    }
+
+    checkMpi(MPI_Finalize(), "MPI_Finalize");
+    return EXIT_SUCCESS;
 }

    20	[[noreturn]] void fail(const char* where, const char* detail) {
    21	    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s: %s\n", worldRank, where, detail);
    22	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);

    26	void checkMpi(const int status, const char* where) {
    27	    if (status != MPI_SUCCESS) {
    28	        char error[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
    29	        int length = 0;
    30	        MPI_Error_string(status, error, &length);
    31	        fail(where, error);
    32	    }
    33	}
    34	
    35	void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* where) {
    36	    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
    37	        fail(where, cudaGetErrorString(status));
    38	    }
    39	}
    40	
    41	void checkCublas(const cublasStatus_t status, const char* where) {
    42	    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
    43	        fail(where, "cuBLAS operation failed");
    44	    }
    45	}
    46	
    47	// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is identical
    48	// to the original initialization, including the global (rather than local)
    49	// row index used by ranks that own a subset of A.
    50	constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    51	    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
    52	           static_cast<double>(N * N);
    53	}
    54	
    55	class PinnedBuffer {
    56	  public:
    57	    PinnedBuffer() = default;
    58	
    59	    explicit PinnedBuffer(const size_t elements) { allocate(elements); }
    60	
    61	    PinnedBuffer(const PinnedBuffer&) = delete;
    62	    PinnedBuffer& operator=(const PinnedBuffer&) = delete;
    63	
    64	    ~PinnedBuffer() {
    65	        if (data_ != nullptr) {
    66	            // There is no useful recovery path while unwinding program exit.
    67	            cudaFreeHost(data_);
    68	        }
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    void allocate(const size_t elements) {
    72	        if (elements == 0) {
    73	            return;
    74	        }
    75	        if (elements > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
    76	            fail("host allocation", "matrix is too large");
    77	        }
    78	        checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), elements * sizeof(double)),
    79	                  "cudaMallocHost");
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    double* data() noexcept { return data_; }
    83	
    84	  private:
    85	    double* data_ = nullptr;
    86	};
    87	
    88	void initMatrixRows(double* const mat, const size_t N, const size_t firstRow,
    89	                    const size_t rowCount) {
    90	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    91	    for (long long localRow = 0; localRow < static_cast<long long>(rowCount); ++localRow) {
    92	        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(localRow);
    93	        double* const row = mat + static_cast<size_t>(localRow) * N;
    94	        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
    95	            row[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, j);
    96	        }
    97	    }
    98	}
    99	
   100	// Validation uses the same summation order and deterministic input values as
   101	// the serial version, while avoiding a second full copy of A on rank zero.
   102	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
   103	    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
   104	
   105	    for (const size_t iPoint : checkPoints) {
   106	        for (const size_t jPoint : checkPoints) {
   107	            const size_t i = iPoint % N;
   108	            const size_t j = jPoint % N;
   109	            double expected = 0.0;
   110	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   111	                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * getPseudoRndValue(N, k, j);
   112	            }
   113	
   114	            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
   115	            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
   116	            if (relError > 1e-6) {
   117	                std::printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f "
   118	                            "(error: %.10e)\n",
   119	                            i, j, expected, actual, relError);
   120	                return false;
   121	            }
   122	        }
   123	    }
   124	    return true;
   125	}
   126	
   127	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   128	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   129	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   130	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
   131	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   132	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   133	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   134	}
   135	
   136	bool parseMatrixSize(const char* text, size_t* const value) {
   137	    char* end = nullptr;
   138	    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
   139	    if (text[0] == '\0' || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
   140	        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
   141	        return false;
   142	    }
   143	    *value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
   144	    return true;
   145	}

   153

   297	        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
   298	                                 static_cast<int>(N), static_cast<int>(localRows),
   299	                                 static_cast<int>(N), &alpha, deviceB, static_cast<int>(N),
   300	                                 deviceA, static_cast<int>(N), &beta, deviceC,
   301	                                 static_cast<int>(N)),
   302	                    "cublasDgemm");
   303	    }
   304	
   305	    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent, stream), "cudaEventRecord(stop)");
   306	    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent), "cudaEventSynchronize(stop)");
   307	    float localMilliseconds = 0.0F;
   308	    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&localMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent),
   309	              "cudaEventElapsedTime");
   310	
   311	    double maximumMilliseconds = 0.0;
   312	    const double localMillisecondsDouble = static_cast<double>(localMilliseconds);
   313	    checkMpi(MPI_Reduce(&localMillisecondsDouble, &maximumMilliseconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX,
   314	                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD),
   315	             "MPI_Reduce(timing)");
   316	
   317	    const bool needFullResult = printResults || validate;
   318	    PinnedBuffer localResult(needFullResult ? localElements : 0);
   319	    std::vector<double> result;
   320	    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
   321	    std::vector<int> receiveDisplacements;
   322	    if (needFullResult) {
   323	        if (localElements != 0) {
   324	            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(localResult.data(), deviceC, localElements * sizeof(double),
   325	                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
   326	                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C)");
   327	            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize(result)");
   328	        }
   329	
   330	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   331	            result.resize(matrixElements);
   332	            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   333	            receiveDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
   334	            size_t displacement = 0;
   335	            for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
   336	                const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
   337	                const size_t count = rows * N;
   338	                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
   339	                receiveDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
   340	                displacement += count;
   341	            }
   342	        }
   343	
   344	        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(localResult.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
   345	                             worldRank == 0 ? result.data() : nullptr,
   346	                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
   347	                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveDisplacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
   348	                             MPI_COMM_WORLD),
   349	                 "MPI_Gatherv(C)");
   350	    }
   351	
   352	    if (worldRank == 0) {
   353	        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", maximumMilliseconds);
   354	        const double gflops = maximumMilliseconds > 0.0
   355	                                  ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) *
   356	                                     static_cast<double>(N)) /
   357	                                        (maximumMilliseconds * 1.0e6)
   358	                                  : 0.0;
   359	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   360	
   361	        if (printResults) {
   362	            print_results(result, "MatrixC");
   363	        }
   364	        if (validate) {
   365	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   366	            if (!validateResult(result, N)) {
   367	                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   368	                checkMpi(MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE), "MPI_Abort(validation)");
   369	                return EXIT_FAILURE;
   370	            }
   371	            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   372	        }
   373	    }
   374	
   375	    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(start)");
   376	    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(stop)");
   377	    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy");
   378	    checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
   379	    if (deviceA != nullptr) {
   380	        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)");

+void checkMpi(const int status, const char* where) {
+    if (status != MPI_SUCCESS) {
+        char error[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
+        int length = 0;

+    }

+void checkCublas(const cublasStatus_t status, const char* where) {

+bool parseMatrixSize(const char* text, size_t* const value) {

+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);

+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;

+            result.resize(matrixElements);
+            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            receiveDisplacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            size_t displacement = 0;
+            for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+                const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraRows ? 1 : 0);
+                const size_t count = rows * N;
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+                receiveDisplacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+                displacement += count;
+            }
+        }
+
+        checkMpi(MPI_Gatherv(localResult.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? result.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                             worldRank == 0 ? receiveDisplacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                             MPI_COMM_WORLD),
+                 "MPI_Gatherv(C)");
+    }
+
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", maximumMilliseconds);
+        const double gflops = maximumMilliseconds > 0.0
+                                  ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                                     static_cast<double>(N)) /
+                                        (maximumMilliseconds * 1.0e6)
+                                  : 0.0;
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+        }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (!validateResult(result, N)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                checkMpi(MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE), "MPI_Abort(validation)");
+                return EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(start)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(stop)");
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy");
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+    if (deviceA != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)");
+    if (deviceC != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)");
+    }
+
+    checkMpi(MPI_Finalize(), "MPI_Finalize");
+    return EXIT_SUCCESS;
 }

codex
Implemented the unconditional MPI + OpenMP + CUDA hybrid matmul.

- MPI distributes contiguous `A` rows across ranks and reduces timing; optional result/validation paths gather `C`.
- OpenMP parallelizes host matrix initialization.
- CUDA/cuBLAS runs optimized DGEMM per GPU, with pinned memory and rank-to-local-GPU mapping.
- Build now requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA; executable remains `matmul`.

Updated [matmul.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_hybrid_r1/matmul/matmul.cpp) and [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_hybrid_r1/matmul/CMakeLists.txt).

Verified with clean CMake builds and MPI runs, including `-np 2 -n 512 -v` (validation passed).

+bool parseMatrixSize(const char* text, size_t* const value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '\0' || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return true;

+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", maximumMilliseconds);
+        const double gflops = maximumMilliseconds > 0.0
+                                  ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                                     static_cast<double>(N)) /
+                                        (maximumMilliseconds * 1.0e6)
+                                  : 0.0;
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(result, "MatrixC");
+        }
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (!validateResult(result, N)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                checkMpi(MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE), "MPI_Abort(validation)");
+                return EXIT_FAILURE;
+            }
+            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(start)");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent), "cudaEventDestroy(stop)");
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy");
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "cudaStreamDestroy");
+    if (deviceA != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)");
+    if (deviceC != nullptr) {
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)");
+    }
+
+    checkMpi(MPI_Finalize(), "MPI_Finalize");
+    return EXIT_SUCCESS;

Implemented the unconditional MPI + OpenMP + CUDA hybrid matmul.

- MPI distributes contiguous `A` rows across ranks and reduces timing; optional result/validation paths gather `C`.
- OpenMP parallelizes host matrix initialization.
- CUDA/cuBLAS runs optimized DGEMM per GPU, with pinned memory and rank-to-local-GPU mapping.
- Build now requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA; executable remains `matmul`.
